##[group]Run f=playwright-report/results.json
f=playwright-report/results.json
if [ ! -f "$f" ]; then echo "no results.json - the run died before writing one"; exit 0; fi
node -e '
  const r = require("./playwright-report/results.json");
  const out = [];
  const walk = (s) => (s.suites ?? []).forEach(walk) ||
    (s.specs ?? []).forEach((sp) => sp.tests.forEach((t) =>
      out.push({ status: t.status, title: sp.title, file: sp.file,
        err: (t.results ?? []).map((x) => x.error?.message).find(Boolean) ?? "" })));
  (r.suites ?? []).forEach(walk);
  const by = (st) => out.filter((o) => o.status === st).length;
  const failed = out.filter((o) => o.status !== "expected" && o.status !== "skipped");
  const line = `${failed.length} failed, ${by("expected")} passed, ${by("skipped")} skipped, ${out.length} total`;
  console.log(line);
  const files = [...new Set(failed.map((f) => f.file))].sort();
  console.log(`${files.length} spec files with failures:`);
  const clean = (e) => e.replace(/\\u001b\\[[0-9;]*m/g, "").split("\
")[0].slice(0, 160);
  for (const f of files) {
    console.log("  " + f);
    for (const t of failed.filter((x) => x.file === f))
      console.log(`      ${t.title}\
        ${clean(t.err) || "(no error message)"}`);
  }
  const sum = process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY;
  if (sum) require("fs").appendFileSync(sum,
    `### E2E\
\
${line}\
\
` +
    files.map((f) => `- ${f}\
` + failed.filter((x) => x.file === f)
      .map((t) => `  - ${t.title} — \\`${clean(t.err) || "no message"}\\``).join("\
")).join("\
") + "\
");
'
